# H09 evidence / Протокол H09

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Reviewed result — PASS (bounded powered Series qualification)

Instrument: Keithley 2231A-30-3, `*IDN?` revision `1.04-0.01` (menu version
reported separately as Main 1.04 / Aux 0.01). Wiring: external CH1−↔CH2+;
KEL103 in CR across CH1+ / CH2−; DMM7510 FRONT DCV 100 V across the same
combined output; CH3 open. Oscilloscope and common-ground bus disconnected.

| Point | Keithley `read_channel(1)` V / A | KEL103 V / A / W | DMM7510 V | Panel review |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CV2 / CR40 | 1.9946 / 0.0496144 (`CV`) | 1.9873 / 0.0493 / 0.0979 | 1.987460 | CV / CR confirmed |
| CC4 / CR12 | 2.99667 / 0.248519 (`UNKNOWN`) | 2.9830 / 0.2484 / 0.7409 | 2.985412 | CC / CR confirmed |
| CV40 / CR2000 | 39.9985 / 0.0195642 (`CV`) | 39.991 / 0.0195 / 0.7798 | 39.993250 | CV / CR confirmed |
| CC40 / CR1600 | 36.9841 / 0.0224762 (`UNKNOWN`) | 36.990 / 0.0226 / 0.8359 | 36.989930 | CC / CR confirmed |

All 632 PSU `SYST:ERR?` replies were `0, "No error"`; all test points
stabilized within the runner's bounded sampling loop. The 40 V CC acceptance
uses 30.0–40.5 V, KEL103 current agreement with DMM voltage / CR1600 within 5%
or 1 mA, a 1 W ceiling, and the PSU/KEL103 current comparison tolerance based
on Series accuracy. The operator confirmed every front-panel mode. At both CC
points, the PSU OPER status decoded as CV (`STAT:OPER:INST:ISUM1:COND?=9`) while
QUES decoded as CC (`STAT:QUES:INST:ISUM1:COND?=2`); the library correctly
preserved the conflict as `regulation=UNKNOWN`. U/I and raw masks were recorded;
the physical CC mode was confirmed on the panel. This is a readback caveat, not
a setpoint or output-control failure.

After the final operator disconnect confirmation, the runner restored the safe
independent profile, verified all outputs OFF, and left KEL103 INPUT OFF. The
operator confirmed the KEL103, DMM7510 and Series jumper were physically
disconnected. This is HW OBSERVED PASS for the listed scope, not a universal
hardware safety guarantee.
